prose-gray dark:prose-invert [contain:inline-size] isolate empty:hidden mb-14" data-page-title="Send messages to a running agent" data-page-href="/en/agent-sdk/streaming-vs-single-mode" id="content"><span data-as="p">Streaming input keeps an Agent SDK session open so your application can send it more than one message while it runs. You can also attach images to a message and stop a response partway through to redirect Claude.</span>
<div role="note" aria-label="Note" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 dark:border-blue-900 dark:bg-blue-600/20" data-callout-type="note"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="size-4 text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300"><use href="#mi-mint-1-CalloutNote"></use></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">Streaming input covers the messages you send. To show Claude’s response as it’s generated, see <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/streaming-output" class="link">Stream responses in real-time</a>.</span></div></div>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="choose-single-shot-or-streaming-input" aria-labelledby="_R_59deehkd74t9_"><a href="#choose-single-shot-or-streaming-input" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Choose single-shot or streaming input"></a><span id="_R_59deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Choose single-shot or streaming input</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Choose streaming input for an application that holds a conversation with Claude, such as a chat interface. Use streaming input to attach images to a message, interrupt a response, or <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/configuration#change-configuration-mid-session" class="link">switch the model or permission mode</a> while the session runs.</span>
<span data-as="p">A single-shot call passes one prompt string to <code>query()</code>. The call ends after Claude answers it. Single-shot fits a single task, such as a serverless function. To follow up after that call ends, start a new call that resumes the session. For more information, see <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/sessions#continue-resume-and-fork" class="link">Continue, resume, and fork</a>.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="send-more-than-one-message-to-a-session" aria-labelledby="_R_b9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#send-more-than-one-message-to-a-session" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Send more than one message to a session"></a><span id="_R_b9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Send more than one message to a session</span></h2>
<ul>
<li><strong>TypeScript</strong>: write an <a href="https://developer.mozilla.org/en-US/docs/Web/JavaScript/Reference/Statements/async_function%2A" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">async generator</a> that yields <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/typescript#sdkusermessage" class="link"><code>SDKUserMessage</code></a> objects, and pass it as the <code>prompt</code> of <code>query()</code>. Make the generator wait between yields for the next message from your user.</li>
<li><strong>Python</strong>: open a <code>ClaudeSDKClient</code> and pass each message to <code>client.query()</code>. Pass a string for a text message. To attach an image, pass an <a href="https://docs.python.org/3/glossary.html#term-asynchronous-generator" class="link" target="_blank" rel="noreferrer">async generator</a> that yields user message dicts.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">To have Claude answer each message in its own response, wait for the previous response’s <a href="#read-each-response" class="link">result message</a> before you send the next one:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>TypeScript</strong>: have your generator await a promise. Resolve it in your <code>for await</code> loop over <code>query()</code> when the result message arrives.</li>
<li><strong>Python</strong>: call <code>client.query()</code> with the next message after your <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/python#claudesdkclient" class="link"><code>receive_response()</code></a> loop ends.</li>
</ul>
<div role="note" aria-label="Note" class="callout my-4 px-5 py-4 overflow-hidden rounded-2xl flex gap-3 border border-blue-200 bg-blue-50 dark:border-blue-900 dark:bg-blue-600/20" data-callout-type="note"><div class="mt-0.5 w-4" data-component-part="callout-icon"><svg aria-hidden="true" width="18" height="18" stroke-width="1.5" class="size-4 text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300"><use href="#mi-mint-1-CalloutNote"></use></svg></div><div class="text-sm prose dark:prose-invert min-w-0 w-full [&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-light dark:[&amp;_kbd]:bg-background-dark [&amp;_code]:text-current! [&amp;_kbd]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:text-current! [&amp;_a]:border-current [&amp;_strong]:text-current! text-blue-800 dark:text-blue-300" data-component-part="callout-content"><span data-as="p">A message you send during tool calls reaches Claude after those tool calls finish. Claude then answers it and your earlier message in one response. If the turn ends first, Claude starts a new turn for it. For details, see <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/agent-loop#turns-and-budget" class="link">Turns and budget</a>.</span></div></div>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="send-a-follow-up-with-an-image" aria-labelledby="_R_l9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#send-a-follow-up-with-an-image" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Send a follow-up with an image"></a><span id="_R_l9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Send a follow-up with an image</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">This example sends a text question, then a follow-up with an image, and prints Claude’s response to each. Before you run it, save a PNG image as <code>diagram.png</code> in your working directory:</span>
<div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" class="code-group p-0.5 mt-5 mb-8 flex flex-col not-prose relative rounded-2xl border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 <span data-as="p">When you run either version, the example prints <code>Response 1 finished: success</code> and <code>Response 2 finished: success</code>.</span>
<span data-as="p">If Claude didn’t see your image, see <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/troubleshooting#image-could-not-be-processed" class="link">Image could not be processed</a>.</span>
<h3 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="feed-messages-from-a-chat-interface-in-typescript" aria-labelledby="_R_v9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#feed-messages-from-a-chat-interface-in-typescript" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Feed messages from a chat interface in TypeScript"></a><span id="_R_v9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Feed messages from a chat interface in TypeScript</span></h3>
<span data-as="p">Feed your user’s messages to your prompt generator through a queue:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong><code>pushMessage()</code></strong>: your interface’s input handler calls this for each message the user sends.</li>
<li><strong><code>closeInput()</code></strong>: the handler calls this when the user closes the chat.</li>
<li><strong><code>userMessages()</code></strong>: the generator you pass as the <code>prompt</code> of <code>query()</code>.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p"><code>pushMessage()</code> adds each message to an array, and the generator reads from that array. When the array is empty, the generator waits until a message arrives or the chat closes:</span>
<div class="code-block mt-5 mb-8 not-prose rounded-2xl relative group min-w-0 print:print-color-exact text-gray-950 dark:text-gray-50 codeblock-light border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 <span data-as="p">Messages you push during a response can share one response. To choose when Claude reads a message you push during a response, set the message’s <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/typescript#sdkusermessage" class="link"><code>priority</code></a> field.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="read-each-response" aria-labelledby="_R_1b9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#read-each-response" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Read each response"></a><span id="_R_1b9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Read each response</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">Claude ends each response with a result message.</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>TypeScript</strong>: the <code>for await</code> loop over <code>query()</code> yields each response’s messages in order. The loop ends after the last response’s result message, once your generator has returned. To match a result to the messages it answers, set a <code>uuid</code> on each message you send, and read the result’s <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/typescript#user_message_uuids" class="link"><code>user_message_uuids</code></a>.</li>
<li><strong>Python</strong>: start a <code>receive_response()</code> loop for each response, because each loop stops after the next <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/python#resultmessage" class="link"><code>ResultMessage</code></a>. The client keeps the conversation’s context from one message to the next. For more information, see <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/sessions#automatic-session-management" class="link">Automatic session management</a>.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">The result message’s <code>subtype</code> field reports how the response ended, such as <code>success</code> or <code>error_during_execution</code>.</span>
<span data-as="p">If the last response ends in an error result:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>TypeScript</strong>: the loop over <code>query()</code> yields that result and then throws. Wrap the loop in <code>try</code>/<code>catch</code> to handle the exception. To keep the conversation going after the throw, start a new <code>query()</code> call that <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/sessions#continue-resume-and-fork" class="link">resumes the session</a>. While your generator still has messages to send, an error result for an earlier response doesn’t throw.</li>
<li><strong>Python</strong>: <code>receive_response()</code> on a <code>ClaudeSDKClient</code> yields the error result without raising.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">For what each result subtype means, see <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/agent-loop#handle-the-result" class="link">Handle the result</a>.</span>
<h2 class="flex whitespace-pre-wrap group font-semibold" id="interrupt-claude’s-response" aria-labelledby="_R_1p9deehkd74t9_"><a href="#interrupt-claude’s-response" class="anchor-link" aria-label="Navigate to header: Interrupt Claude’s response"></a><span id="_R_1p9deehkd74t9_" class="cursor-pointer">Interrupt Claude’s response</span></h2>
<span data-as="p">To stop a response partway through and keep the session open, call <code>interrupt()</code> on the object that runs the session:</span>
<ul>
<li><strong>TypeScript</strong>: pass an async generator as <code>prompt</code>, save the <a href="/docs/en/agent-sdk/typescript#query-object" class="link"><code>Query</code> object</a> returned by <code>query()</code>, and call <code>interrupt()</code> on it.</li>
<li><strong>Python</strong>: call <code>interrupt()</code> on the <code>ClaudeSDKClient</code>. If a <code>receive_response()</code> loop is reading the response, that loop ends at the interrupted response’s <code>ResultMessage</code>. Send your next message after that result arrives.</li>
</ul>
<span data-as="p">When you call <code>interrupt()</code> during a response, that response ends with an <code>error_during_execution</code> result. Messages you sent before the interrupt that Claude hadn’t read yet still reach Claude once the interrupted response ends. For what the TypeScript loop does when the last response is an error result, see <a href="#read-each-response" class="link">Read each response</a>.</span>
<span data-as="p">This example asks Claude for a long essay. The example calls <code>interrupt()</code> three seconds after the session starts, then sends a second message to the same session once the interrupted response ends:</span>
<div data-orientation="horizontal" data-activation-direction="none" class="code-group p-0.5 mt-5 mb-8 flex flex-col not-prose relative rounded-2xl border border-gray-950/10 dark:border-white/10 <span data-as="p">When you run either version, the first response stops partway through the essay, and the example prints <code>Response 1 finished: error_during_execution</code>. Claude then answers the second message. The output ends with <code>Response 2 finished: success</code>.</span>
